Theorem 221.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let the riemann_surfaces structure S satisfy the conditions of convergence analysis framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 146. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 353 and converges at rate O(1/n^4).

At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 353. QED.
